import os
import dj_database_url
from .settings import *

SECRET_KEY = os.environ.get("DJANGO_SECRET_KEY", SECRET_KEY)
DEBUG = os.environ.get("DJANGO_DEBUG", "False").lower() == "true"

_hosts = os.environ.get("DJANGO_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "*")
ALLOWED_HOSTS = [h.strip() for h in _hosts.split(",") if h.strip()] or ["*"]

_csrf = os.environ.get("DJANGO_CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS", "")
if _csrf:
    CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS = [u.strip() for u in _csrf.split(",") if u.strip()]

if os.environ.get("DATABASE_URL"):
    DATABASES = {
        "default": dj_database_url.config(conn_max_age=600, ssl_require=False)
    }

MIDDLEWARE.insert(1, "whitenoise.middleware.WhiteNoiseMiddleware")
STATIC_ROOT = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "staticfiles")
STATICFILES_STORAGE = "whitenoise.storage.CompressedManifestStaticFilesStorage"

_redis = os.environ.get("REDIS_URL", "")
if _redis:
    CHANNEL_LAYERS = {
        "default": {
            "BACKEND": "channels_redis.core.RedisChannelLayer",
            "CONFIG": {"hosts": [_redis]},
        }
    }

SECURE_PROXY_SSL_HEADER = ("HTTP_X_FORWARDED_PROTO", "https")

from django.contrib import admin
from django.urls import include, path

from accounts.urls import web_urlpatterns as accounts_web
from accounts.views import landing
from config.beta_views import beta_lock, beta_logout


urlpatterns = [
    # Beta Gate
    path("beta-lock/", beta_lock, name="beta_lock"),
    path("beta-logout/", beta_logout, name="beta_logout"),

    # پنل ادمین پیش‌فرض Django
    path("django-admin/", admin.site.urls),

    # پنل ادمین سفارشی
    path("panel-admin/", include("accounts.admin_urls")),

    # API
    path("api/auth/", include("accounts.urls")),

    # Web (با namespace "accounts")
    path("accounts/", include((accounts_web, "accounts"), namespace="accounts")),

    # سایر اپ‌ها
    path("game/", include("game.urls")),
    path("social/", include("social.urls")),
    path("alliance/", include("alliance.urls")),

    # ریشه → landing
    path("", landing, name="landing"),
]

===== FILE: ./config/beta_gate.py =====
"""محافظ Beta برای دسترسی محدود در فاز آزمایشی."""

from django.shortcuts import redirect


BETA_PASSWORD = "emami_irani_esm1405"

EXEMPT_PATHS = [
    "/beta-lock/",
    "/beta-logout/",
    "/static/",
    "/favicon.ico",
]


class BetaGateMiddleware:
    def __init__(self, get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response

    def __call__(self, request):
        path = request.path
        for exempt in EXEMPT_PATHS:
            if path.startswith(exempt):
                return self.get_response(request)
        if request.session.get("beta_unlocked") is True:
            return self.get_response(request)
        return redirect(f"/beta-lock/?next={path}")

import game.routing

application = ProtocolTypeRouter({
    "http": django_asgi_app,
    "websocket": AuthMiddlewareStack(
        URLRouter(game.routing.websocket_urlpatterns)
    ),
})

===== FILE: ./config/beta_views.py =====
"""صفحات Beta Gate."""

from django.shortcuts import redirect, render
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt

from .beta_gate import BETA_PASSWORD


@csrf_exempt
def beta_lock(request):
    if request.session.get("beta_unlocked") is True:
        next_url = request.GET.get("next", "/") or "/"
        return redirect(next_url)

    error = None
    next_url = request.GET.get("next", "/") or "/"

    if request.method == "POST":
        entered = (request.POST.get("password") or "").strip()
        next_url = request.POST.get("next", "/") or "/"

        if entered == BETA_PASSWORD:
            request.session["beta_unlocked"] = True
            request.session.set_expiry(60 * 60 * 24 * 7)
            return redirect(next_url)
        else:
            error = "رمز اشتباه است. دوباره تلاش کن."

    return render(request, "beta_lock.html", {"error": error, "next": next_url})


def beta_logout(request):
    request.session.pop("beta_unlocked", None)
    return redirect("beta_lock")

<script>
// ============================================================
// ۱) اعمال تنظیمات ذخیره شده در هنگام بارگذاری هر صفحه
// ============================================================
(function applySettings() {
  // حالت تاریک/روشن
  const theme = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'dark';
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
  document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
  
  // اندازه فونت
  const fontSize = localStorage.getItem('fontSize') || 'medium';
  document.documentElement.classList.remove('font-small', 'font-medium', 'font-large');
  document.documentElement.classList.add('font-' + fontSize);
  
  // قطع انیمیشن‌ها
  const animOn = localStorage.getItem('animations') !== 'false';
  if (!animOn) {
    document.documentElement.classList.add('no-animations');
  } else {
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('no-animations');
  }
})();

// ============================================================
// ۲) موسیقی و افکت‌های صوتی
// ============================================================
const _soundOn = () => localStorage.getItem('sound') !== 'false';
const _musicOn = () => localStorage.getItem('music') !== 'false';
const _soundVol = () => (parseInt(localStorage.getItem('soundVol')) || 80) / 100;
const _musicVol = () => (parseInt(localStorage.getItem('musicVol')) || 50) / 100;
const _vibOn = () => localStorage.getItem('vibration') !== 'false';

let _audioCtx = null;
function _getCtx() {
  if (!_audioCtx) {
    try { _audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); }
    catch(e) { return null; }
  }
  return _audioCtx;
}

function playBeep(freq = 1000, duration = 0.05, type = 'sine') {
  if (!_soundOn()) return;
  const vol = _soundVol();
  if (vol <= 0) return;
  try {
    const ctx = _getCtx();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = type;
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * 0.15, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + duration);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + duration);
  } catch(e) {}
}

// صداهای از پیش تعریف شده
window.soundClick = () => playBeep(1200, 0.04);
window.soundTab = () => playBeep(900, 0.05);
window.soundSuccess = () => { playBeep(880, 0.08); setTimeout(() => playBeep(1320, 0.1), 80); };
window.soundError = () => playBeep(300, 0.15, 'square');
window.soundTick = (urgent) => playBeep(urgent ? 1000 : 800, 0.06);
window.soundBack = () => { playBeep(600, 0.05); setTimeout(() => playBeep(400, 0.06), 50); };

window.vibrate = (ms = 50) => {
  if (_vibOn() && navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(ms);
};

// صدا روی کلیک هر عنصر
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const target = e.target.closest('a, button, .mode-card, .menu-item, .pill, .cat-tag, .back-btn');
  if (target) {
    if (target.classList.contains('back-btn')) {
      window.soundBack();
    } else {
      window.soundClick();
    }
  }
}, true);

// ============================================================
// ۳) مدیریت موسیقی پس‌زمینه
// ============================================================
const bgMusic = document.getElementById('globalMusic');
let _musicStarted = false;

function _startMusic() {
  if (_musicStarted || !bgMusic) return;
  if (!_musicOn()) return;
  bgMusic.volume = _musicVol();
  bgMusic.play().catch(() => {});
  _musicStarted = true;
}

// شروع موسیقی در اولین کلیک/لمس کاربر
document.addEventListener('click', _startMusic, { once: true });
document.addEventListener('touchstart', _startMusic, { once: true });

// اگه تنظیمات از صفحه دیگه‌ای عوض شد، اینجا هم اعمال بشه
window.addEventListener('storage', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'music') {
    if (_musicOn() && bgMusic) {
      bgMusic.volume = _musicVol();
      bgMusic.play().catch(() => {});
    } else if (bgMusic) {
      bgMusic.pause();
    }
  }
  if (e.key === 'musicVol' && bgMusic) {
    bgMusic.volume = _musicVol();
  }
  if (e.key === 'theme') {
    const t = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'dark';
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', t);
    document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', t);
  }
  if (e.key === 'fontSize') {
    const fs = localStorage.getItem('fontSize') || 'medium';
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('font-small', 'font-medium', 'font-large');
    document.documentElement.classList.add('font-' + fs);
  }
  if (e.key === 'animations') {
    if (localStorage.getItem('animations') === 'false') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('no-animations');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('no-animations');
    }
  }
});

// هندلر مخصوص صفحه تنظیمات برای اعمال فوری
window.applySettingsNow = function(key, value) {
  if (key === 'theme') {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', value);
    document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', value);
  }
  if (key === 'fontSize') {
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('font-small', 'font-medium', 'font-large');
    document.documentElement.classList.add('font-' + value);
  }
  if (key === 'animations') {
    if (value === 'false') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('no-animations');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('no-animations');
    }
  }
  if (key === 'music') {
    if (value === 'true' && bgMusic) {
      bgMusic.volume = _musicVol();
      bgMusic.play().catch(() => {});
      _musicStarted = true;
    } else if (bgMusic) {
      bgMusic.pause();
    }
  }
  if (key === 'musicVol' && bgMusic) {
    bgMusic.volume = parseInt(value) / 100;
  }
};
</script>

</html>

===== FILE: ./make_avatars.py =====
import os
import ssl
import time
import random
import urllib.request

out_dir = "static/avatars/generated"
os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

# SSL context که به خطاها حساس نباشه
ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
ctx.check_hostname = False
ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE

# پارامترها
tops = ["ShortHairShortFlat", "ShortHairShortRound", "ShortHairTheCaesar",
        "LongHairBob", "LongHairBun", "LongHairCurly", "LongHairStraight",
        "LongHairFro", "LongHairMiaWallace", "LongHairNotTooLong",
        "Hijab", "Turban", "Hat", "WinterHat1", "WinterHat2",
        "NoHair", "ShortHairDreads01", "ShortHairFrizzle"]
facial = ["Blank", "BeardMedium", "BeardLight", "BeardMajestic",
          "MoustacheFancy", "MoustacheMagnum"]
access = ["Blank", "Round", "Sunglasses", "Wayfarers", "Prescription01"]
skin = ["edb98a", "d08b5b", "614335", "ffdbb4", "ae5d29", "fd9841", "f8d25c"]
hair_c = ["2c1b18", "4a312c", "724133", "a55728", "b58143",
          "c93305", "d6b370", "e8e1e1", "f59797"]
cloth_c = ["262e33", "65c9ff", "5199e4", "25557c", "e6e6e6",
           "929598", "3c4f5c", "b1e2ff", "a7ffc4", "ffafb9"]
cloth_t = ["BlazerShirt", "Hoodie", "ShirtCrewNeck", "ShirtVNeck", "Overall"]
eyes = ["Default", "Happy", "Side", "Squint", "Wink", "Surprised"]
mouth = ["Smile", "Serious", "Default", "Twinkle", "Grimace"]

random.seed(42)
seen = set()
combos = []
while len(combos) < 100:
    key = (random.choice(tops), random.choice(facial), random.choice(access), random.choice(skin))
    if key not in seen:
        seen.add(key)
        combos.append(key)

def build_url(t, f, a, s):
    return (
        f"https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/png?"
        f"topType={t}&accessoriesType={a}&hairColor={random.choice(hair_c)}"
        f"&facialHairType={f}&clotheType={random.choice(cloth_t)}"
        f"&clotheColor={random.choice(cloth_c)}&eyeType={random.choice(eyes)}"
        f"&eyebrowType=Default&mouthType={random.choice(mouth)}"
        f"&skinColor={s}&size=300&radius=50"
    )

def try_download(url, path, max_retries=5):
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
            data = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20, context=ctx).read()
            if len(data) > 200:
                with open(path, "wb") as fp:
                    fp.write(data)
                return True
        except Exception as e:
            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                time.sleep(1 + attempt)  # وقفه پیش‌رونده
                continue
        return False
    return False

print("Downloading 100 avatars...")
ok = 0
fail_list = []

for i, (t, f, a, s) in enumerate(combos, 1):
    path = f"{out_dir}/{i:03d}.png"

    # اگه قبلاً دانلود شده، رد شو
    if os.path.exists(path) and os.path.getsize(path) > 200:
        ok += 1
        print(f"  {i}/100 ⏭ (already)")
        continue

    url = build_url(t, f, a, s)
    if try_download(url, path):
        ok += 1
        print(f"  {i}/100 ✓")
    else:
        fail_list.append(i)
        print(f"  {i}/100 ✗")

print(f"\n✅ {ok}/100 دانلود شد")

if fail_list:
    print(f"\n⚠️ {len(fail_list)} تا ناموفق — دوباره اجرا کن:")
    print("   python3 make_avatars.py")

===== FILE: ./social/views.py =====
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.db.models import Q, Sum, Count
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.utils import timezone
from datetime import timedelta
import random

from accounts.avatars import get_emoji
from game.models import Game, Player
from .models import Friendship, Notification, GameInvite

User = get_user_model()

def _avatar_of(user):
    return get_emoji(user.avatar_id)

@login_required
def friends_home(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")

    now = timezone.now()
    online_threshold = now - timedelta(minutes=5)
    
    # آپدیت وضعیت آنلاین بودن کاربر فعلی
    User.objects.filter(id=request.user.id).update(last_seen=now)

    # دوستان تایید شده
    accepted = Friendship.objects.filter(
        Q(from_user=request.user, status="accepted") |
        Q(to_user=request.user, status="accepted")
    ).select_related("from_user", "to_user")

    friends = []
    for f in accepted:
        other = f.to_user if f.from_user_id == request.user.id else f.from_user
        is_online = other.last_seen and other.last_seen >= online_threshold
        
        stats = Player.objects.filter(user=other).aggregate(
            total_score=Sum('score'), games=Count('id')
        )
        total_score = stats['total_score'] or 0
        games = stats['games'] or 0
        level = 1 + total_score // 100

        friends.append({
            "user": other,
            "avatar": _avatar_of(other),
            "friendship_id": f.id,
            "is_online": is_online,
            "level": level,
            "total_score": total_score,
            "games": games,
        })

    # درخواست‌های دریافتی
    incoming = Friendship.objects.filter(to_user=request.user, status="pending").select_related("from_user")
    incoming_list = [{"req": f, "from": f.from_user, "avatar": _avatar_of(f.from_user)} for f in incoming]

    # درخواست‌های ارسالی
    outgoing = Friendship.objects.filter(from_user=request.user, status="pending").select_related("to_user")
    outgoing_list = [{"req": f, "to": f.to_user, "avatar": _avatar_of(f.to_user)} for f in outgoing]

    # دعوت‌نامه‌های بازی در انتظار
    pending_invites = GameInvite.objects.filter(to_user=request.user, status="pending").select_related("from_user", "game")

    return render(request, "social/friends.html", {
        "friends": friends,
        "incoming": incoming_list,
        "outgoing": outgoing_list,
        "pending_invites": pending_invites,
        "active": "friends",
    })

@login_required
def search_user(request):
    query = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    results = []
    error = None

    if query:
        # جستجو با نام نمایشی یا نام کاربری
        users = User.objects.filter(
            Q(alias__icontains=query) | Q(username__icontains=query)
        ).exclude(id=request.user.id)

        for u in users[:20]:
            fs = Friendship.objects.filter(
                Q(from_user=request.user, to_user=u) |
                Q(from_user=u, to_user=request.user)
            ).first()

            state = "none"
            if fs:
                if fs.status == "accepted":
                    state = "friend"
                elif fs.status == "pending":
                    state = "pending_out" if fs.from_user_id == request.user.id else "pending_in"

            results.append({
                "user": u,
                "avatar": _avatar_of(u),
                "state": state,
            })

        if not results:
            error = "کاربری با این مشخصات پیدا نشد."

    return render(request, "social/search.html", {
        "query": query,
        "results": results,
        "error": error,
    })

@login_required
def send_request(request, user_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("social:friends_home")
    to_user = get_object_or_404(User, id=user_id)
    if to_user == request.user:
        return redirect("social:friends_home")

    existing = Friendship.objects.filter(
        Q(from_user=request.user, to_user=to_user) | Q(from_user=to_user, to_user=request.user)
    ).first()

    if existing:
        if existing.status == "rejected":
            existing.status = "pending"
            existing.from_user = request.user
            existing.to_user = to_user
            existing.save()
    else:
        Friendship.objects.create(from_user=request.user, to_user=to_user)

    return redirect(request.META.get("HTTP_REFERER") or "social:friends_home")

@login_required
def accept_request(request, req_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("social:friends_home")
    req = get_object_or_404(Friendship, id=req_id, to_user=request.user, status="pending")
    req.status = "accepted"
    req.save()
    return redirect("social:friends_home")

@login_required
def reject_request(request, req_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("social:friends_home")
    req = get_object_or_404(Friendship, id=req_id, to_user=request.user, status="pending")
    req.status = "rejected"
    req.save()
    return redirect("social:friends_home")

@login_required
def remove_friend(request, user_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("social:friends_home")
    other = get_object_or_404(User, id=user_id)
    Friendship.objects.filter(
        Q(from_user=request.user, to_user=other) | Q(from_user=other, to_user=request.user)
    ).delete()
    return redirect("social:friends_home")

@login_required
def chat_view(request, user_id):
    other = get_object_or_404(User, id=user_id)
    return render(request, "social/chat.html", {
        "other": other,
        "other_avatar": _avatar_of(other),
        "my_avatar": _avatar_of(request.user),
    })

# سیستم دعوت به بازی
# ============================================================

@login_required
def send_game_invite(request, user_id):
    if request.method == "POST":
        to_user = get_object_or_404(User, id=user_id)
        
        categories = request.POST.getlist("categories")
        if not categories: categories = ["اسم", "فامیل", "میوه"]
        letter_mode = request.POST.get("letter_mode", "random")
        letter = request.POST.get("letter", "").strip()
        if letter_mode == "random" or not letter:
            letter = random.choice(list("ابپتثجچحخدذرزژسشصضطظعغفقکگلمنوهی"))

        game = Game.objects.create(
            host=request.user,
            status="waiting",
            categories=categories,
            mode="invite",
        )
        Player.objects.create(game=game, user=request.user)

        inv = GameInvite.objects.create(
            from_user=request.user,
            to_user=to_user,
            game=game,
        )

        # چک آنلاین بودن
        is_online = to_user.last_seen and to_user.last_seen >= (timezone.now() - timedelta(minutes=5))

        Notification.objects.create(
            user=to_user,
            type="game_invite",
            title="دعوت به بازی",
            body=f"{request.user.alias} شما را به بازی دعوت کرد!",
            url="/social/"
        )

        return JsonResponse({"status": "ok", "is_online": is_online, "invite_id": inv.id})
    return JsonResponse({"status": "error"}, status=400)

@login_required
def check_invite_status(request, invite_id):
    inv = get_object_or_404(GameInvite, id=invite_id, from_user=request.user)
    if inv.status == "accepted":
        return JsonResponse({"status": "accepted", "url": f"/game/room/{inv.game.code}/"})
    elif inv.status == "rejected":
        return JsonResponse({"status": "rejected"})
    return JsonResponse({"status": "pending"})

@login_required
def accept_invite(request, invite_id):
    if request.method == "POST":
        inv = get_object_or_404(GameInvite, id=invite_id, to_user=request.user, status="pending")
        inv.status = "accepted"
        inv.save()
        Player.objects.get_or_create(game=inv.game, user=request.user)
        return JsonResponse({"status": "ok", "url": f"/game/room/{inv.game.code}/"})
    return JsonResponse({"status": "error"}, status=400)

@login_required
def reject_invite(request, invite_id):
    if request.method == "POST":
        inv = get_object_or_404(GameInvite, id=invite_id, to_user=request.user, status="pending")
        inv.status = "rejected"
        inv.save()
        return JsonResponse({"status": "ok"})
    return JsonResponse({"status": "error"}, status=400)

===== FILE: ./social/models.py =====
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models


class Friendship(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("pending", "در انتظار"),
        ("accepted", "پذیرفته شده"),
        ("rejected", "رد شده"),
    ]

    from_user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="sent_friend_requests",
    )
    to_user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="received_friend_requests",
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="pending")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("from_user", "to_user")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.from_user.alias} -> {self.to_user.alias} ({self.status})"


class Notification(models.Model):
    TYPES = [
        ("friend_request", "درخواست دوستی"),
        ("friend_accepted", "قبول دوستی"),
        ("message", "پیام جدید"),
        ("game_invite", "دعوت به بازی"),
        ("game_result", "نتیجه بازی"),
        ("alliance_invite", "دعوت به اتحاد"),
    ]

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="notifications",
    )
    type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TYPES)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    body = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)
    url = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ("-created_at",)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.alias} — {self.title}"


class GameInvite(models.Model):
    from_user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="sent_invites")
    to_user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="received_invites")
    game = models.ForeignKey("game.Game", on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, default="pending")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.from_user.alias} -> {self.to_user.alias} ({self.status})"

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
// لیست کامل دسته‌ها
const ALL_CATS = [
    "اسم", "فامیل", "میوه", "رنگ", "شهر", "کشور", "ماشین", "حیوان", "اشیا", "غذا",
    "شغل", "اعضای بدن", "نام گل", "فیلم", "خواننده", "برند", "ورزش", "ابزار", "لباس", 
    "شخصیت کارتونی", "کتاب", "مکان", "گیاه", "مزه", "صدا", "پارچه", "بیماری", 
    "زبان", "عدد", "کلمه انگلیسی"
];

let currentTargetId = null;
let pollInterval = null;
let currentLetterMode = 'random';

// ساخت دسته‌ها هنگام لود صفحه
const catsContainer = document.getElementById('inviteCats');
ALL_CATS.forEach(cat => {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'cat-opt active';
    el.innerText = cat;
    el.onclick = () => {
        el.classList.toggle('active');
        if (typeof window.soundClick === 'function') window.soundClick();
    };
    catsContainer.appendChild(el);
});

function openInviteModal(userId, name, isOnline) {
  currentTargetId = userId;
  document.getElementById('targetName').innerText = name;
  document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.add('active');
  
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnSendInvite');
  if (!isOnline) {
    btn.innerText = 'دوستت آفلاینه! پیام در صف می‌مونه';
    btn.classList.add('offline');
  } else {
    btn.innerText = 'ارسال درخواست بازی';
    btn.classList.remove('offline');
  }
}

function closeInviteModal() {
  document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.remove('active');
  if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
}

function setLetterMode(mode) {
  currentLetterMode = mode;
  document.getElementById('btnModeRandom').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('btnModeManual').classList.remove('active');
  
  if (mode === 'random') {
    document.getElementById('btnModeRandom').classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('inviteLetter').classList.remove('show');
  } else {
    document.getElementById('btnModeManual').classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('inviteLetter').classList.add('show');
    document.getElementById('inviteLetter').focus();
  }
  if (typeof window.soundClick === 'function') window.soundClick();
}

function sendInviteRequest() {
  const letter = document.getElementById('inviteLetter').value.trim();
  const selectedCats = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#inviteCats .cat-opt.active')).map(el => el.innerText);
  
  if (selectedCats.length === 0) {
    alert('حداقل یک دسته را انتخاب کنید!');
    return;
  }

  const formData = new FormData();
  formData.append('letter_mode', currentLetterMode);
  formData.append('letter', currentLetterMode === 'manual' ? letter : '');
  selectedCats.forEach(c => formData.append('categories', c));
  formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnSendInvite');
  const originalText = btn.innerText;
  btn.innerText = 'در حال ارسال...';

  fetch(`/social/invite/${currentTargetId}/`, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: formData
  }).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
    if (data.status === 'ok') {
      if (data.is_online) {
        btn.innerText = '⏳ منتظر قبول کردن...';
        pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
          fetch(`/social/invite/status/${data.invite_id}/`).then(r=>r.json()).then(st => {
            if (st.status === 'accepted') {
              clearInterval(pollInterval);
              window.location.href = st.url;
            } else if (st.status === 'rejected') {
              alert('دعوت رد شد.');
              closeInviteModal();
            }
          });
        }, 2000);
      } else {
        alert('دوستت آفلاین است. به محض ورود، درخواستت را می‌بیند.');
        closeInviteModal();
      }
    } else {
      alert('خطا در ارسال درخواست');
      btn.innerText = originalText;
    }
  }).catch(() => {
    alert('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    btn.innerText = originalText;
  });
}

function acceptInvite(inviteId) {
  fetch(`/social/invite/accept/${inviteId}/`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': '{{ csrf_token }}' } })
  .then(r=>r.json()).then(data => {
    if(data.status === 'ok') window.location.href = data.url;
  });
}

function rejectInvite(inviteId) {
  fetch(`/social/invite/reject/${inviteId}/`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': '{{ csrf_token }}' } })
  .then(() => window.location.reload());
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">

    body {

    }

    .chat-head {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 14px;

    }
    .chat-head .av {

    path("reject/<int:req_id>/", views.reject_request, name="reject_request"),

===== FILE: ./social/notifications.py =====
from .models import Notification


def notify(user, ntype, title, body="", url=""):
    """ساخت اعلان جدید."""
    try:
        return Notification.objects.create(
            user=user, type=ntype, title=title, body=body, url=url,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None


def unread_count(user):
    if not user.is_authenticated:
        return 0
    return Notification.objects.filter(user=user, is_read=False).count()

===== FILE: ./social/admin.py =====
from django.contrib import admin
from .models import Friendship, Notification


@admin.register(Friendship)
class FriendshipAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("from_user", "to_user", "status", "created_at")
    list_filter = ("status",)
    search_fields = ("from_user__alias", "to_user__alias")


@admin.register(Notification)
class NotificationAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("user", "type", "title", "is_read", "created_at")
    list_filter = ("type", "is_read")
    search_fields = ("user__alias", "title")

===== FILE: ./alliance/views.py =====
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render

from accounts.avatars import get_emoji
from .models import Alliance, AllianceMember, AllianceMessage


def _avatar(user):
    return get_emoji(user.avatar_id)


@login_required
def alliance_home(request):
    """صفحه اتحاد: اگر کاربر عضو است، به پروفایل اتحاد، وگرنه به ساخت/ورود."""
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")

    my_membership = AllianceMember.objects.filter(user=request.user).select_related("alliance").first()
    if my_membership:
        return redirect("alliance:detail", alliance_id=my_membership.alliance.id)

    # لیست اتحادهای عمومی (برای مرور)
    alliances = Alliance.objects.all().order_by("-created_at")[:20]
    alliances_data = []
    for a in alliances:
        alliances_data.append({
            "obj": a,
            "count": a.member_count(),
            "leader_avatar": _avatar(a.leader),
        })

    return render(request, "alliance/home.html", {
        "alliances": alliances_data,
        "active": "alliance",
    })


@login_required
def create_alliance(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")

    if AllianceMember.objects.filter(user=request.user).exists():
        messages.info(request, "شما الان عضو یک اتحاد هستید.")
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    error = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        name = (request.POST.get("name") or "").strip()[:40]
        tag = (request.POST.get("tag") or "").strip().upper()[:8]
        description = (request.POST.get("description") or "").strip()[:200]
        emblem = (request.POST.get("emblem") or "🛡️").strip()[:4]

        if not name:
            error = "نام اتحاد را وارد کن."
        elif len(name) < 3:
            error = "نام اتحاد باید حداقل ۳ حرف باشد."
        elif Alliance.objects.filter(name=name).exists():
            error = "این نام قبلاً گرفته شده."
        else:
            a = Alliance.objects.create(
                name=name, tag=tag, description=description,
                emblem=emblem or "🛡️", leader=request.user,
            )
            AllianceMember.objects.create(alliance=a, user=request.user, role="leader")
            messages.success(request, f"اتحاد «{name}» ساخته شد!")
            return redirect("alliance:detail", alliance_id=a.id)

    emojis = ["🛡️", "⚔️", "🏰", "🐉", "🦅", "🐺", "🔥", "⚡", "👑", "💎", "🌟", "🦁"]
    return render(request, "alliance/create.html", {
        "error": error,
        "emojis": emojis,
    })


@login_required
def join_by_code(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")

    if AllianceMember.objects.filter(user=request.user).exists():
        messages.info(request, "شما الان عضو یک اتحاد هستید.")
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    error = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        code = (request.POST.get("code") or "").strip().upper()
        a = Alliance.objects.filter(invite_code=code).first()
        if not a:
            error = "کد دعوت نامعتبر است."
        elif a.member_count() >= a.max_members:
            error = "این اتحاد پر است."
        else:
            AllianceMember.objects.create(alliance=a, user=request.user, role="member")
            messages.success(request, f"به اتحاد «{a.name}» پیوستی!")
            return redirect("alliance:detail", alliance_id=a.id)

    return render(request, "alliance/join.html", {"error": error})


@login_required
def alliance_detail(request, alliance_id):
    a = get_object_or_404(Alliance, id=alliance_id)
    my_membership = AllianceMember.objects.filter(alliance=a, user=request.user).first()

    if not my_membership:
        messages.info(request, "برای دیدن اتحاد باید عضو شوی.")
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    from django.utils import timezone
    from datetime import timedelta
    from django.db.models import Sum, Count
    from game.models import Player

    now = timezone.now()
    online_threshold = now - timedelta(minutes=5)

    members = AllianceMember.objects.filter(alliance=a).select_related("user").order_by("role", "-joined_at")
    members_data = []
    for m in members:
        is_online = m.user.last_seen and m.user.last_seen >= online_threshold
        stats = Player.objects.filter(user=m.user).aggregate(total_score=Sum("score"))
        total_score = stats["total_score"] or 0
        level = 1 + total_score // 100

        members_data.append({
            "user": m.user,
            "avatar": _avatar(m.user),
            "role": m.role,
            "role_display": m.get_role_display(),
            "joined_at": m.joined_at,
            "is_online": is_online,
            "level": level,
        })

    recent_messages = AllianceMessage.objects.filter(alliance=a).select_related("sender").order_by("-sent_at")[:5]
    recent_messages = list(reversed(recent_messages))

    return render(request, "alliance/detail.html", {
        "alliance": a,
        "members": members_data,
        "my_role": my_membership.role,
        "is_leader": my_membership.role == "leader",
        "recent_messages": recent_messages,
        "member_count": len(members_data),
    })


@login_required
def alliance_chat(request, alliance_id):
    a = get_object_or_404(Alliance, id=alliance_id)
    my_membership = AllianceMember.objects.filter(alliance=a, user=request.user).first()
    if not my_membership:
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    if request.method == "POST":
        body = (request.POST.get("body") or "").strip()[:500]
        if body:
            AllianceMessage.objects.create(alliance=a, sender=request.user, body=body)
        return redirect("alliance:chat", alliance_id=a.id)

    all_messages = AllianceMessage.objects.filter(alliance=a).select_related("sender").order_by("sent_at")[:200]
    messages_data = []
    for m in all_messages:
        messages_data.append({
            "sender": m.sender,
            "avatar": _avatar(m.sender),
            "body": m.body,
            "sent_at": m.sent_at,
            "is_me": m.sender_id == request.user.id,
        })

    return render(request, "alliance/chat.html", {
        "alliance": a,
        "messages": messages_data,
    })


@login_required
def leave_alliance(request, alliance_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    a = get_object_or_404(Alliance, id=alliance_id)
    m = AllianceMember.objects.filter(alliance=a, user=request.user).first()
    if not m:
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    if m.role == "leader":
        # اگر رهبر تنها عضو است، اتحاد پاک شود
        if a.member_count() <= 1:
            a.delete()
            messages.info(request, "اتحاد پاک شد.")
            return redirect("alliance:home")
        else:
            messages.error(request, "رهبر نمی‌تواند اتحاد را ترک کند. ابتدا رهبری را منتقل کن.")
            return redirect("alliance:detail", alliance_id=a.id)

    m.delete()
    messages.info(request, "از اتحاد خارج شدی.")
    return redirect("alliance:home")


@login_required
def kick_member(request, alliance_id, user_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("alliance:home")

    a = get_object_or_404(Alliance, id=alliance_id)
    my_m = AllianceMember.objects.filter(alliance=a, user=request.user).first()

    if not my_m or my_m.role != "leader":
        messages.error(request, "دسترسی نداری.")
        return redirect("alliance:detail", alliance_id=a.id)

    target = AllianceMember.objects.filter(alliance=a, user_id=user_id).first()
    if target and target.role != "leader":
        target.delete()
        messages.success(request, "عضو حذف شد.")

    return redirect("alliance:detail", alliance_id=a.id)

===== FILE: ./alliance/models.py =====
import random
import string

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone


def _generate_invite_code():
    while True:
        code = "".join(random.choices(string.ascii_uppercase + string.digits, k=6))
        if not Alliance.objects.filter(invite_code=code).exists():
            return code


class Alliance(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=40, unique=True)
    tag = models.CharField(max_length=8, blank=True)  # مثلا [IRN]
    description = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    emblem = models.CharField(max_length=4, default="🛡️")  # emoji
    invite_code = models.CharField(max_length=6, unique=True, editable=False)
    leader = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="led_alliances",
    )
    max_members = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=30)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.invite_code:
            self.invite_code = _generate_invite_code()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def member_count(self):
        return self.members.count()

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.emblem} {self.name}"


class AllianceMember(models.Model):
    ROLE_CHOICES = [
        ("leader", "رهبر"),
        ("officer", "افسر"),
        ("member", "عضو"),
    ]

    alliance = models.ForeignKey(Alliance, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="members")
    user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="alliance_memberships")
    role = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=ROLE_CHOICES, default="member")
    joined_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("alliance", "user")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.alias} in {self.alliance.name} ({self.role})"


class AllianceMessage(models.Model):
    alliance = models.ForeignKey(Alliance, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="messages")
    sender = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    body = models.CharField(max_length=500)
    sent_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ("sent_at",)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.sender.alias}: {self.body[:30]}"

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
function copyCode() {
  const code = document.getElementById('codeText').innerText;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(code).then(() => {
    if (typeof window.soundSuccess === 'function') window.soundSuccess();
    alert('کد دعوت کپی شد: ' + code);
  }).catch(() => {
    alert('کد دعوت: ' + code);
  });
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== FILE: ./alliance/urls.py =====
from django.urls import path
from . import views

app_name = "alliance"

urlpatterns = [
    path("", views.alliance_home, name="home"),
    path("create/", views.create_alliance, name="create"),
    path("join/", views.join_by_code, name="join"),
    path("<int:alliance_id>/", views.alliance_detail, name="detail"),
    path("<int:alliance_id>/chat/", views.alliance_chat, name="chat"),
    path("<int:alliance_id>/leave/", views.leave_alliance, name="leave"),
    path("<int:alliance_id>/kick/<int:user_id>/", views.kick_member, name="kick"),
]

                ('role', models.CharField(choices=[('leader', 'رهبر'), ('officer', 'افسر'), ('member', 'عضو')], default='member', max_length=10)),
                ('joined_at', models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)),
                ('alliance', models.ForeignKey(on_delete=django.db.models.deletion.CASCADE, related_name='members', to='alliance.alliance')),
                ('user', models.ForeignKey(on_delete=django.db.models.deletion.CASCADE, related_name='alliance_memberships', to=settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL)),
            ],
            options={
                'unique_together': {('alliance', 'user')},
            },
        ),
    ]

import secrets
import random

from django.contrib.auth import authenticate, get_user_model, login, logout
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.db.models import Avg, Max, Sum
from django.shortcuts import redirect, render
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import generics, permissions
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView
from rest_framework_simplejwt.views import TokenObtainPairView

from .avatars import AVATAR_CATEGORIES, IMAGE_AVATARS, get_emoji, get_avatar_display
from .models import EmailVerificationToken
from .serializers import LoginSerializer, RegisterSerializer, UserSerializer

# ============================================================
#                       API Endpoints
# ============================================================
class RegisterView(generics.CreateAPIView):
    serializer_class = RegisterSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]

class LoginView(TokenObtainPairView):
    serializer_class = LoginSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]

class MeView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
    serializer_class = UserSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    def get_object(self):
        return self.request.user

class VerifyEmailView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]
    def get(self, request):
        token = request.query_params.get("token", "")
        try:
            ev = EmailVerificationToken.objects.select_related("user").get(token=token)
        except EmailVerificationToken.DoesNotExist:
            return Response({"detail": "invalid token"}, status=400)
        if not ev.is_valid():
            return Response({"detail": "expired or used"}, status=400)
        ev.used_at = timezone.now()
        ev.save(update_fields=["used_at"])
        user = ev.user
        user.is_active = True
        user.email_verified_at = timezone.now()
        user.save(update_fields=["is_active", "email_verified_at"])
        return Response({"detail": "email verified"})

class ResendVerificationView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.AllowAny]
    def post(self, request):
        User = get_user_model()
        email = (request.data.get("email") or "").lower()
        try:
            user = User.objects.get(email=email)
            if not user.is_active:
                pass
        except User.DoesNotExist:
            pass
        return Response({"detail": "if the email exists, we sent a link"})

# ============================================================
#                       Web (HTML)
# ============================================================
def _next_after_login(user):
    if not user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")
    if not user.has_completed_registration:
        return redirect("accounts:complete_registration")
    return redirect("game:home")

def landing(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return _next_after_login(request.user)
    return render(request, "accounts/landing.html")

def register_view(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return _next_after_login(request.user)
    error = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = (request.POST.get("username") or "").strip().lower()
        password = request.POST.get("password") or ""
        password2 = request.POST.get("password2") or ""
        User = get_user_model()
        if not username:
            error = "شناسه کاربری را وارد کن."
        elif len(username) < 3:
            error = "شناسه کاربری باید حداقل ۳ کاراکتر باشد."
        elif len(username) > 32:
            error = "شناسه کاربری نمی‌تواند بیشتر از ۳۲ کاراکتر باشد."
        elif not all(c.isalnum() or c == "_" for c in username):
            error = "شناسه کاربری فقط می‌تواند شامل حروف انگلیسی، اعداد و _ باشد."
        elif User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            error = "این شناسه کاربری قبلاً استفاده شده است."
        elif len(password) < 6:
            error = "رمز عبور باید حداقل ۶ کاراکتر باشد."
        elif password != password2:
            error = "دو رمز عبور یکسان نیستند."
        else:
            user = User.objects.create_user(username=username, password=password, alias=username, is_active=True)
            login(request, user, backend="django.contrib.auth.backends.ModelBackend")
            return redirect("accounts:complete_registration")
    return render(request, "accounts/register.html", {"error": error})

def login_view(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return _next_after_login(request.user)
    error = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = (request.POST.get("username") or "").strip().lower()
        password = request.POST.get("password") or ""
        user = authenticate(request, username=username, password=password)
        if user is not None:
            login(request, user)
            return _next_after_login(user)
        error = "شناسه کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است."
    return render(request, "accounts/login.html", {"error": error})

def guest_start(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect("game:home")
    
    User = get_user_model()
    for _ in range(30):
        num = random.randint(10_000_000, 99_999_999)
        username = f"user_{num}"
        if not User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            break
    
    password = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        username=username, password=password, alias=username,
        avatar_id=None, has_chosen_avatar=True, has_completed_registration=True,
        is_active=True, is_guest=True,
    )
    login(request, user, backend="django.contrib.auth.backends.ModelBackend")
    return redirect("game:home")

def guest_name(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        new_alias = (request.POST.get("alias") or "").strip()[:32]
        if new_alias and new_alias != request.user.alias:
            User = get_user_model()
            if not User.objects.filter(alias__iexact=new_alias).exclude(pk=request.user.pk).exists():
                request.user.alias = new_alias
                request.user.save(update_fields=["alias"])
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")
    return render(request, "accounts/guest_name.html")

@login_required
def complete_registration(request):
    user = request.user
    if user.has_completed_registration:
        return redirect("game:home")
    error = None
    if request.method == "POST":
        alias = (request.POST.get("alias") or "").strip()[:32]
        avatar_type = request.POST.get("avatar_type", "emoji")
        if avatar_type not in ("emoji", "image"):
            avatar_type = "emoji"
        avatar_raw = request.POST.get("avatar_id", "")
        
        try:
            avatar_id = int(avatar_raw)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            avatar_id = -1
            
        max_id = 99 if avatar_type == "emoji" else 44

        if not alias:
            error = "نام نمایشی را وارد کن."
        elif len(alias) < 2:
            error = "نام نمایشی باید حداقل ۲ کاراکتر باشد."
        elif not (0 <= avatar_id <= max_id):
            error = "لطفاً یک آواتار انتخاب کن."
        else:
            user.alias = alias
            user.avatar_id = avatar_id
            user.avatar_type = avatar_type
            user.has_chosen_avatar = True
            user.has_completed_registration = True
            user.save(update_fields=["alias", "avatar_id", "avatar_type", "has_chosen_avatar", "has_completed_registration"])
            return redirect("game:home")

    return render(request, "accounts/complete_registration.html", {
        "categories": AVATAR_CATEGORIES,
        "image_avatars": IMAGE_AVATARS,
        "current_avatar": user.avatar_id,
        "current_type": user.avatar_type,
        "error": error,
    })

@login_required
def avatar_select(request):
    if request.user.is_guest:
        return render(request, "accounts/avatar_select.html", {
            "categories": AVATAR_CATEGORIES,
            "image_avatars": IMAGE_AVATARS,
            "is_guest": True,
            "locked_message": "🔒 تغییر آواتار در حالت مهمان قفل است.",
        })
    if request.method == "POST":
        avatar_type = request.POST.get("avatar_type", "emoji")
        if avatar_type not in ("emoji", "image"):
            avatar_type = "emoji"
        try:
            aid = int(request.POST.get("avatar_id", ""))
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            aid = -1
            
        max_id = 99 if avatar_type == "emoji" else 44
        
        if 0 <= aid <= max_id:
            request.user.avatar_id = aid
            request.user.avatar_type = avatar_type
            request.user.has_chosen_avatar = True
            request.user.save(update_fields=["avatar_id", "avatar_type", "has_chosen_avatar"])
            return redirect("accounts:profile")

    return render(request, "accounts/avatar_select.html", {
        "categories": AVATAR_CATEGORIES,
        "image_avatars": IMAGE_AVATARS,
        "current_avatar": request.user.avatar_id,
        "current_type": request.user.avatar_type,
        "is_guest": False,
    })

def logout_view(request):
    logout(request)
    return redirect("landing")

@login_required
def profile_view(request):
    from game.models import Player
    user = request.user
    message = None
    error = None

    if request.method == "POST" and user.is_guest:
        error = "🔒 این قابلیت در حالت مهمان قفل است."
    elif request.method == "POST":
        new_alias = (request.POST.get("alias") or "").strip()[:32]
        if new_alias and new_alias != user.alias:
            if len(new_alias) < 2:
                error = "نام نمایشی باید حداقل ۲ کاراکتر باشد."
            else:
                user.alias = new_alias
                user.save(update_fields=["alias"])
                message = "نام نمایشی تغییر کرد."

    my_players = Player.objects.filter(user=user)
    total_games = my_players.count()
    total_score = my_players.aggregate(t=Sum("score"))["t"] or 0
    best_score = my_players.aggregate(m=Max("score"))["m"] or 0
    avg_score = my_players.aggregate(a=Avg("score"))["a"] or 0
    avg_score = round(avg_score, 1)

    level = 1 + total_score // 100
    level_progress = total_score % 100
    
    atype, aval = get_avatar_display(user)
    
    return render(request, "accounts/profile.html", {
        "profile_user": user,
        "avatar_emoji": get_emoji(user.avatar_id),
        "avatar_type": atype,
        "avatar_value": aval,
        "message": message,
        "error": error,
        "is_guest": user.is_guest,
        "active": "profile",
        "level": level,
        "level_progress": level_progress,
        "level_progress_text": f"{level_progress} / 100 امتیاز تا لول بعد",
        "stats": {
            "total_games": total_games,
            "total_score": total_score,
            "best_score": best_score,
            "avg_score": avg_score,
        },
    })

@login_required
def settings_view(request):
    return render(request, "accounts/settings.html", {"active": "profile"})

@login_required
def shop_view(request):
    return render(request, "accounts/shop.html", {"active": "profile"})

===== FILE: ./accounts/serializers.py =====
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from rest_framework import serializers
from rest_framework_simplejwt.serializers import TokenObtainPairSerializer

User = get_user_model()


class UserSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ("id", "email", "alias", "public_id", "date_joined")
        read_only_fields = fields


class RegisterSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, min_length=8)

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ("email", "alias", "password")

    def validate_email(self, value):
        value = value.lower()
        if User.objects.filter(email=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("This email is already registered.")
        return value

    def validate_alias(self, value):
        if User.objects.filter(alias__iexact=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("This alias is already taken.")
        return value

    def create(self, validated_data):
        return User.objects.create_user(**validated_data)


class LoginSerializer(TokenObtainPairSerializer):
    """Adds the user object to the standard JWT response."""

    def validate(self, attrs):
        data = super().validate(attrs)
        data["user"] = UserSerializer(self.user).data
        return data

    {"key": "objects", "name": "اشیا", "items": ["💎", "🔮", "🎩", "👑", "🎸", "🎺", "🥁", "🎨", "📚", "✏️"]},
    {"key": "abstract", "name": "انتزاعی", "items": ["🌀", "💠", "🔱", "⚛️", "♻️", "🔰", "✴️", "❇️", "✨", "💫"]},
]

ALL_AVATARS = []
AVATAR_BY_ID = {}
for _cat in AVATAR_CATEGORIES:
    for _i, _emoji in enumerate(_cat["items"]):
        _avatar_id = len(ALL_AVATARS)
        ALL_AVATARS.append({"id": _avatar_id, "category": _cat["key"], "category_name": _cat["name"], "emoji": _emoji})
        AVATAR_BY_ID[_avatar_id] = _emoji

def get_emoji(avatar_id):
    if avatar_id is None: return "👤"
    try: return AVATAR_BY_ID.get(int(avatar_id), "👤")
    except (ValueError, TypeError): return "👤"

def get_image_url(avatar_id):
    if avatar_id is None: return None
    try: aid = int(avatar_id)
    except (ValueError, TypeError): return None
    if not (1 <= aid <= 44): return None
    return f"/static/avatars/images/{aid:03d}.jpg"

def get_avatar_display(user):
    if user is None: return ("emoji", "👤")
    avatar_type = getattr(user, "avatar_type", "emoji") or "emoji"
    avatar_id = getattr(user, "avatar_id", None)
    if avatar_type == "image":
        url = get_image_url(avatar_id)
        if url: return ("image", url)
        return ("emoji", "👤")
    return ("emoji", get_emoji(avatar_id))

# ۴۴ عکس لوکال
IMAGE_AVATARS = [{"id": i, "url": f"/static/avatars/images/{i:03d}.jpg"} for i in range(1, 45)]

===== FILE: ./accounts/emails.py =====
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.mail import send_mail

from .models import EmailVerificationToken


def send_verification_email(user) -> str:
    ev = EmailVerificationToken.issue(user)
    link = f"{settings.BACKEND_URL}/api/auth/verify-email/?token={ev.token}"
    send_mail(
        subject="تأیید ایمیل — اسم‌فامیل آنلاین",
        message=f"برای تأیید ایمیل این لینک را باز کنید:\n\n{link}\n\nاین لینک ۲۴ ساعت معتبر است.",
        from_email=settings.DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,
        recipient_list=[user.email],
        fail_silently=False,
    )
    return ev.token
===== FILE: ./accounts/models.py =====
import random
import secrets
from datetime import timedelta

from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractBaseUser, BaseUserManager, PermissionsMixin
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone


def _generate_public_id():
    while True:
        candidate = random.randint(100_000_000, 999_999_999)
        if not User.objects.filter(public_id=candidate).exists():
            return candidate


class UserManager(BaseUserManager):
    def create_user(self, username, password=None, **extra):
        if not username:
            raise ValueError("نام کاربری الزامی است")
        username = username.strip().lower()
        user = self.model(username=username, **extra)
        user.set_password(password)
        user.save(using=self._db)
        return user

    def create_superuser(self, username, password=None, **extra):
        extra.setdefault("is_staff", True)
        extra.setdefault("is_superuser", True)
        extra.setdefault("is_active", True)
        extra.setdefault("has_chosen_avatar", True)
        extra.setdefault("has_completed_registration", True)
        extra.setdefault("alias", username)
        extra.setdefault("email_verified_at", timezone.now())
        return self.create_user(username, password, **extra)


class User(AbstractBaseUser, PermissionsMixin):
    # ===== اطلاعات ورود =====
    username = models.CharField(max_length=32, unique=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, null=True, blank=True)
    email = models.EmailField(null=True, blank=True)

    # ===== پروفایل =====
    alias = models.CharField(max_length=32, blank=True)  # نام نمایشی
    public_id = models.PositiveBigIntegerField(unique=True, editable=False)
    avatar_id = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    avatar_type = models.CharField(max_length=8, default="emoji")  # emoji | image
    has_chosen_avatar = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    has_completed_registration = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_guest = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    # ===== تنظیمات =====
    settings_json = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)

    # ===== وضعیت =====
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    email_verified_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    is_staff = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    date_joined = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    last_seen = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)

    objects = UserManager()

    USERNAME_FIELD = "username"
    REQUIRED_FIELDS = []

    DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
        "sound": True,
        "music": True,
        "sound_vol": 80,
        "music_vol": 50,
        "notif_invite": True,
        "notif_friend": True,
        "notif_round": True,
        "theme": "dark",
        "font_size": "medium",
        "animations": True,
        "vibration": True,
    }

    def get_settings(self):
        s = dict(self.DEFAULT_SETTINGS)
        s.update(self.settings_json or {})
        return s

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.public_id:
            self.public_id = _generate_public_id()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.username} ({self.public_id})"


class EmailVerificationToken(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="email_tokens")
    token = models.CharField(max_length=64, unique=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    used_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    @classmethod
    def issue(cls, user):
        return cls.objects.create(user=user, token=secrets.token_urlsafe(32))

    def is_valid(self):
        if self.used_at:
            return False
        return timezone.now() < self.created_at + timedelta(hours=24)

        <div class="grid">

              <input type="radio" name="avatar_pick" id="av-{{ cat.key }}-{{ forloop.counter }}" value="{{ cat.key }}-{{ forloop.counter }}" data-emoji="{{ emoji }}">

            </div>

<script>
const catOrder = [
  {% for cat in categories %}"{{ cat.key }}"{% if not forloop.last %},{% endif %}{% endfor %}
];

document.querySelectorAll('input[name="avatar_pick"]').forEach(r => {
  r.addEventListener('change', () => {
    const [catKey, idxStr] = r.value.split('-');
    const catIdx = catOrder.indexOf(catKey);
    const itemIdx = parseInt(idxStr, 10) - 1;
    const realId = catIdx * 10 + itemIdx;
    document.getElementById('avatar_id').value = realId;
  });
});
</script>
</body>

{% block content %}
  <div class="page-head">
    <h1>🎨 تکمیل پروفایل</h1>
    <p>یک نام نمایشی و آواتار برای خودت انتخاب کن.</p>
  </div>
  {% if error %}<div style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15); color:#fca5a5; padding:12px; border-radius:12px; margin-bottom:16px; text-align:center;">{{ error }}</div>{% endif %}
  <form method="post" id="regForm">
    {% csrf_token %}
    <input type="hidden" name="avatar_type" id="avatarType" value="emoji">
    <input type="hidden" name="avatar_id" id="avatarId" value="">
    <div class="box">
      <h3>👤 نام نمایشی</h3>
      <input type="text" name="alias" maxlength="32" required value="{{ request.POST.alias|default:user.alias }}" placeholder="مثلاً: علی">
    </div>
    <div class="box">
      <h3>🎨 آواتار</h3>
      <div class="tabs">
        <button type="button" class="tab active" onclick="switchTab('emoji')">😊 ایموجی</button>
        <button type="button" class="tab" onclick="switchTab('image')">🖼️ شخصیت‌ها</button>
      </div>
      <div id="tab-emoji" class="tab-content active">
        <div class="avatar-grid">
          {% for cat in categories %}
            {% for emoji in cat.items %}
              <div class="avatar-item">
                <input type="radio" name="pick_emoji" id="em-{{ cat.key }}-{{ forloop.counter }}" data-cat="{{ cat.key }}" data-idx="{{ forloop.counter0 }}" onchange="pickEmoji(this)">
                <label for="em-{{ cat.key }}-{{ forloop.counter }}">{{ emoji }}</label>
              </div>
            {% endfor %}
          {% endfor %}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="tab-image" class="tab-content">
        <div class="avatar-grid">
          {% for img in image_avatars %}
            <div class="avatar-item">
              <input type="radio" name="pick_image" id="im-{{ img.id }}" data-id="{{ img.id }}" onchange="pickImage(this)">
              <label for="im-{{ img.id }}" style="background-image: url('{{ img.url }}');"></label>
            </div>
          {% endfor %}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <button type="submit" class="submit" onclick="prepareSubmit()">✅ تمام! بریم بازی</button>
  </form>
{% endblock %}
{% block extra_js %}
<script>
const catOrder = [{% for cat in categories %}"{{ cat.key }}"{% if not forloop.last %},{% endif %}{% endfor %}];
function switchTab(type) {
  document.getElementById('avatarType').value = type;
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  if (type === 'emoji') { document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[0].classList.add('active'); document.getElementById('tab-emoji').classList.add('active'); }
  else { document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[1].classList.add('active'); document.getElementById('tab-image').classList.add('active'); }
  document.getElementById('avatarId').value = '';
}
function pickEmoji(el) { document.getElementById('avatarId').value = catOrder.indexOf(el.dataset.cat) * 10 + parseInt(el.dataset.idx); document.getElementById('avatarType').value = 'emoji'; }
function pickImage(el) { document.getElementById('avatarId').value = parseInt(el.dataset.id); document.getElementById('avatarType').value = 'image'; }
function prepareSubmit() { if (!document.getElementById('avatarId').value) { alert('لطفاً یک آواتار انتخاب کنید!'); event.preventDefault(); } }
</script>
{% endblock %}

            {% endfor %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
const catOrder = [{% for cat in categories %}"{{ cat.key }}"{% if not forloop.last %},{% endif %}{% endfor %}];
function switchTab(type) {
  document.getElementById('avatarType').value = type;
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  if (type === 'emoji') { document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[0].classList.add('active'); document.getElementById('tab-emoji').classList.add('active'); }
  else { document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[1].classList.add('active'); document.getElementById('tab-image').classList.add('active'); }
  document.getElementById('avatarId').value = '';
}
function pickEmoji(el) { document.getElementById('avatarId').value = catOrder.indexOf(el.dataset.cat) * 10 + parseInt(el.dataset.idx); document.getElementById('avatarType').value = 'emoji'; }
function pickImage(el) { document.getElementById('avatarId').value = parseInt(el.dataset.id); document.getElementById('avatarType').value = 'image'; }
function prepareSubmit() { if (!document.getElementById('avatarId').value) { alert('لطفاً یک آواتار انتخاب کنید!'); event.preventDefault(); } }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
// ============================================
// بارگذاری تنظیمات فعلی و اعمال روی فرم
// ============================================
const prefs = {
  sound: localStorage.getItem('sound') !== 'false',
  music: localStorage.getItem('music') !== 'false',
  soundVol: parseInt(localStorage.getItem('soundVol')) || 80,
  musicVol: parseInt(localStorage.getItem('musicVol')) || 50,
  vibration: localStorage.getItem('vibration') !== 'false',
  theme: localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'dark',
  animations: localStorage.getItem('animations') !== 'false',
  notifInvite: localStorage.getItem('notif_invite') !== 'false',
  notifFriend: localStorage.getItem('notif_friend') !== 'false',
  fontSize: localStorage.getItem('fontSize') || 'medium'
};

document.getElementById('tglSound').checked = prefs.sound;
document.getElementById('volSound').value = prefs.soundVol;
document.getElementById('valSound').innerText = prefs.soundVol + '%';

document.getElementById('tglMusic').checked = prefs.music;
document.getElementById('volMusic').value = prefs.musicVol;
document.getElementById('valMusic').innerText = prefs.musicVol + '%';

document.getElementById('tglVib').checked = prefs.vibration;
document.getElementById('tglTheme').checked = (prefs.theme === 'dark');
document.getElementById('tglAnim').checked = prefs.animations;
document.getElementById('tglNotifInvite').checked = prefs.notifInvite;
document.getElementById('tglNotifFriend').checked = prefs.notifFriend;

// اعمال دکمه فعال برای فونت
if (prefs.fontSize === 'small') document.getElementById('btnFontSmall').classList.add('active');
else if (prefs.fontSize === 'large') document.getElementById('btnFontLrg').classList.add('active');
else document.getElementById('btnFontMed').classList.add('active');

// ============================================
// توابع ذخیره آنی
// ============================================
function showToast() {
  const el = document.getElementById('statusMsg');
  el.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), 1500);
}

function saveSetting(key, val) {
  localStorage.setItem(key, val);
  showToast();
  
  if (key === 'sound' && val === true) {
    playBeep();
  }
  if (key === 'music') {
    const bg = document.getElementById('globalMusic');
    if (val && bg) bg.play().catch(e=>e); 
    else if (bg) bg.pause();
  }
  if (key === 'animations') {
    if (!val) {
      document.body.classList.add('no-animations');
      const style = document.createElement('style');
      style.innerHTML = '* { animation: none !important; transition: none !important; }';
      style.id = 'no-anim-style';
      document.head.appendChild(style);
    } else {
      const style = document.getElementById('no-anim-style');
      if (style) style.remove();
    }
  }
}

function updateVol(key, val) {
  localStorage.setItem(key, val);
  document.getElementById(key === 'soundVol' ? 'valSound' : 'valMusic').innerText = val + '%';
  
  if (key === 'soundVol') {
    playBeep();
  } else {
    const bg = document.getElementById('globalMusic');
    if (bg) bg.volume = val / 100;
  }
  showToast();
}

function playBeep() {
  if (!document.getElementById('tglSound').checked) return;
  const vol = document.getElementById('volSound').value / 100;
  try {
    const ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.frequency.value = 1000;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * 0.1, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
    osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(); osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
  } catch(e) {}
}

function changeTheme(isDark) {
  const theme = isDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
  localStorage.setItem('theme', theme);
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
  document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
  showToast();
}

function changeFont(size, btn) {
  localStorage.setItem('fontSize', size);
  document.querySelectorAll('.btn-opt').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  
  if (size === 'small') document.documentElement.style.fontSize = '13px';
  else if (size === 'large') document.documentElement.style.fontSize = '17px';
  else document.documentElement.style.fontSize = '15px';
  
  showToast();
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
function toggleProfileExpand(e) {
  if (e.target.closest('form, a, button')) return;
  const el = document.getElementById('profileHead');
  el.classList.toggle('expanded');
  if (typeof tabSound === 'function') tabSound();
}

function shopSoonAlert() {
  alert('🛒 فروشگاه به‌زودی فعال می‌شود!');
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== FILE: ./accounts/admin_views.py =====
"""پنل ادمین سفارشی — فقط برای مدیر سیستم."""
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db.models import Count, Sum
from django.http import HttpResponse
from django.shortcuts import redirect, render
from django.views.decorators.cache import never_cache


# ⚠️ فقط از اینجا قابل تغییر است — نه از دیتابیس
ADMIN_USERNAME = "admin74"
ADMIN_PASSWORD = "Amir2255ds"


def _check_auth(request):
    """آیا این نشست لاگین ادمین است؟"""
    return request.session.get("custom_admin_logged_in") is True


@never_cache
def admin_login(request):
    """صفحه ورود ادمین."""
    if _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    error = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        username = (request.POST.get("username") or "").strip()
        password = request.POST.get("password") or ""

        if username == ADMIN_USERNAME and password == ADMIN_PASSWORD:
            request.session["custom_admin_logged_in"] = True
            request.session.set_expiry(60 * 60 * 8)
            return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")
        else:
            error = "نام کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است."

    return render(request, "custom_admin/login.html", {"error": error})


@never_cache
def admin_logout(request):
    request.session.pop("custom_admin_logged_in", None)
    return redirect("custom_admin:login")


@never_cache
def dashboard(request):
    """داشبورد ادمین: لیست کاربران."""
    if not _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:login")

    User = get_user_model()
    from game.models import Player

    search = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()

    users_qs = User.objects.all().order_by("-date_joined")

    if search:
        from django.db.models import Q
        users_qs = users_qs.filter(
            Q(username__icontains=search) |
            Q(alias__icontains=search) |
            Q(public_id__icontains=search)
        )

    players_stats = (
        Player.objects
        .values("user_id")
        .annotate(total_score=Sum("score"), games=Count("id"))
    )
    stats_map = {s["user_id"]: s for s in players_stats}

    users_data = []
    for u in users_qs[:500]:
        st = stats_map.get(u.id, {})
        total = st.get("total_score", 0) or 0
        level = 1 + total // 100

        # الگوریتم هش پسورد
        algo = "—"
        if u.password:
            parts = u.password.split("$")
            if parts:
                algo = parts[0]  # argon2 / pbkdf2 / bcrypt

        users_data.append({
            "id": u.id,
            "username": u.username,
            "alias": u.alias,
            "public_id": u.public_id,
            "avatar": u.avatar_id,
            "avatar_type": getattr(u, "avatar_type", "emoji"),
            "level": level,
            "total_score": total,
            "games": st.get("games", 0),
            "is_active": u.is_active,
            "is_staff": u.is_staff,
            "is_guest": getattr(u, "is_guest", False),
            "has_completed": u.has_completed_registration,
            "phone": u.phone or "—",
            "date_joined": u.date_joined,
            "last_login": u.last_login,
            "password_algo": algo,
        })

    total_users = User.objects.count()
    total_players = Player.objects.count()
    total_scores = Player.objects.aggregate(t=Sum("score"))["t"] or 0

    return render(request, "custom_admin/dashboard.html", {
        "users": users_data,
        "search": search,
        "stats": {
            "total_users": total_users,
            "total_games": total_players,
            "total_scores": total_scores,
        },
    })


@never_cache
def user_detail(request, user_id):
    """جزئیات یک کاربر."""
    if not _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:login")

    User = get_user_model()
    try:
        u = User.objects.get(id=user_id)
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    from game.models import Player
    players = Player.objects.filter(user=u).select_related("game").order_by("-id")[:20]

    games_data = []
    for p in players:
        games_data.append({
            "game_id": p.game.id,
            "mode": p.game.mode,
            "score": p.score,
            "created_at": p.game.created_at,
            "status": p.game.status,
        })

    message = request.session.pop("admin_message", None)

    return render(request, "custom_admin/user_detail.html", {
        "u": u,
        "games": games_data,
        "message": message,
    })


@never_cache
def user_toggle_active(request, user_id):
    """فعال/غیرفعال کردن کاربر."""
    if not _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:login")

    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    User = get_user_model()
    try:
        u = User.objects.get(id=user_id)
        u.is_active = not u.is_active
        u.save(update_fields=["is_active"])
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        pass

    return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")


@never_cache
def user_delete(request, user_id):
    """حذف کاربر."""
    if not _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:login")

    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    User = get_user_model()
    try:
        u = User.objects.get(id=user_id)
        u.delete()
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        pass

    return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")


@never_cache
def user_reset_password(request, user_id):
    """تغییر پسورد کاربر توسط ادمین."""
    if not _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:login")

    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    User = get_user_model()
    try:
        u = User.objects.get(id=user_id)
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    new_password = (request.POST.get("new_password") or "").strip()

    if len(new_password) < 6:
        request.session["admin_message"] = {
            "type": "error",
            "text": "رمز جدید باید حداقل ۶ کاراکتر باشد."
        }
    else:
        u.set_password(new_password)
        u.save()
        request.session["admin_message"] = {
            "type": "success",
            "text": f"رمز کاربر {u.username} با موفقیت تغییر کرد."
        }

    return redirect("custom_admin:user_detail", user_id=u.id)


@never_cache
def user_change_alias(request, user_id):
    """تغییر نام نمایشی کاربر توسط ادمین."""
    if not _check_auth(request):
        return redirect("custom_admin:login")

    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    User = get_user_model()
    try:
        u = User.objects.get(id=user_id)
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        return redirect("custom_admin:dashboard")

    new_alias = (request.POST.get("new_alias") or "").strip()[:32]

    if len(new_alias) < 2:
        request.session["admin_message"] = {
            "type": "error",
            "text": "نام نمایشی باید حداقل ۲ کاراکتر باشد."
        }
    else:
        u.alias = new_alias
        u.save(update_fields=["alias"])
        request.session["admin_message"] = {
            "type": "success",
            "text": f"نام نمایشی به «{new_alias}» تغییر کرد."
        }

    return redirect("custom_admin:user_detail", user_id=u.id)

===== FILE: ./accounts/admin.py =====
from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth.admin import UserAdmin as BaseUserAdmin

from .models import User


@admin.register(User)
class UserAdmin(BaseUserAdmin):
    list_display = ("email", "alias", "username", "is_active", "email_verified_at", "is_staff")
    search_fields = ("email", "alias", "username")
    ordering = ("-date_joined",)

    readonly_fields = ("date_joined", "email_verified_at")

    fieldsets = (
        (None, {"fields": ("email", "password")}),
        ("Profile", {"fields": ("alias", "username")}),
        (
            "Permissions",
            {
                "fields": (
                    "is_active",
                    "email_verified_at",
                    "is_staff",
                    "is_superuser",
                    "groups",
                    "user_permissions",
                )
            },
        ),
        ("Timestamps", {"fields": ("date_joined",)}),
    )

    add_fieldsets = (
        (
            None,
            {
                "classes": ("wide",),
                "fields": ("email", "alias", "password1", "password2"),
            },
        ),
    )
