from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Q
import random

from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import redirect, render
from django.utils import timezone

from accounts.avatars import get_emoji

from . import bot as bot_logic
from .bot_data import LETTER_DIFFICULTY
def _require_complete(user):
    """بررسی می‌کند که کاربر ثبت‌نامش را کامل کرده یا نه."""
    if not user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect("landing")
    if not user.has_completed_registration or not user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:complete_registration")
    return None


from .models import (
    DEFAULT_CATEGORIES, Answer, BotAnswer, Game, Player, Round,
)


def _avatar(user):
    from accounts.avatars import get_avatar_display
    _, val = get_avatar_display(user)
    return val


def _normalize(text):
    if not text:
        return ""
    t = text.strip().replace("\u200c", "").replace(" ", "")
    return t.replace("ي", "ی").replace("ك", "ک")


@login_required
def home(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")
    from social.notifications import unread_count
    return render(request, "game/menu.html", {
        "avatar_emoji": _avatar(request.user),
        "active": "home",
        "unread_notifications": unread_count(request.user),
    })


@login_required
def rooms_list(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")
    my_rooms = Game.objects.filter(players__user=request.user).order_by("-created_at")[:5]
    return render(request, "game/rooms.html", {
        "categories": DEFAULT_CATEGORIES,
        "my_rooms": my_rooms,
        "active": "games",
    })


@login_required
def friends_page(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")
    return render(request, "game/coming_soon.html", {
        "icon": "👥",
        "title": "دوستان",
        "message": "این بخش به‌زودی فعال می‌شه.",
    })


@login_required
def quick_play(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")
    return redirect("game:bot_setup")


@login_required
def bot_setup(request):
    if not request.user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:avatar_select")

    if request.method == "POST":
        difficulty = request.POST.get("difficulty", "medium")
        if difficulty not in ("easy", "medium", "hard"):
            difficulty = "medium"

        letter_mode = request.POST.get("letter_mode", "random")
        if letter_mode not in ("random", "player"):
            letter_mode = "random"

        selected = [c for c in request.POST.getlist("categories") if c in DEFAULT_CATEGORIES]
        if not selected:
            selected = DEFAULT_CATEGORIES

        if letter_mode == "player":
            letter = (request.POST.get("letter") or "").strip()[:2]
            if not letter:
                letter = bot_logic.bot_pick_letter(difficulty)
        else:
            letter = bot_logic.bot_pick_letter(difficulty)

        base_time = {"easy": 150, "medium": 120, "hard": 90}[difficulty]
        letter_hardness = LETTER_DIFFICULTY.get(letter, 1)
        duration = base_time + (letter_hardness - 1) * 20

        game = Game.objects.create(
            host=request.user,
            status="in_round",
            categories=selected,
            mode="bot",
            difficulty=difficulty,
            letter_mode=letter_mode,
        )
        Player.objects.create(game=game, user=request.user)
        Round.objects.create(
            game=game,
            number=1,
            letter=letter,
            duration=duration,
        )
        return redirect("game:bot_play", game_id=game.id)

    # دسته‌های غیرمعمول که پیش‌فرض غیرفعالن
    unusual_cats = ["فیلم", "برند", "ورزش", "ابزار", "لباس", "کتاب", "مکان", "گیاه", "بیماری"]
    
    # خوندن تنظیمات کاربر
    user_settings = request.user.settings_json or {}
    # اگه کاربر تنظیمات ذخیره نکرده باشه، اینا پیش‌فرض غیرفعال میشن
    if "excluded_categories" not in user_settings:
        excluded = unusual_cats
    else:
        excluded = user_settings.get("excluded_categories", [])

    main_cats = ["اسم", "فامیل", "میوه", "رنگ", "شهر", "کشور", "ماشین", "حیوان", "اشیا", "غذا", "شغل", "اعضای بدن"]
    
    active_main = [c for c in main_cats if c not in excluded]
    active_extra = [c for c in unusual_cats if c not in excluded]

    return render(request, "game/bot_setup.html", {
        "active_main": active_main,
        "active_extra": active_extra,
        "excluded_categories": excluded,
    })


@login_required
def bot_play(request, game_id):
    game = Game.objects.filter(id=game_id, host=request.user, mode="bot").first()
    if not game:
        return redirect("game:home")
    round_obj = game.rounds.order_by("-number").first()
    if not round_obj:
        return redirect("game:home")
    return render(request, "game/bot_play.html", {
        "game": game,
        "round": round_obj,
        "categories": game.categories,
        "duration": round_obj.duration,
    })


@login_required
def bot_submit(request, game_id):
    game = Game.objects.filter(id=game_id, host=request.user, mode="bot").first()
    if not game:
        return redirect("game:home")
    round_obj = game.rounds.order_by("-number").first()
    if not round_obj or round_obj.stopped_at:
        return redirect("game:home")

    round_obj.stopped_at = timezone.now()
    round_obj.stopper = request.user
    round_obj.save(update_fields=["stopped_at", "stopper"])

    from .bot_data import is_valid_word

    player_answers = []
    for cat in game.categories:
        raw = (request.POST.get(f"cat_{cat}") or "").strip()[:64]
        norm = _normalize(raw)
        score = 0
        reason = ""
        
        if not raw:
            reason = "خالی"
        elif not norm or norm[0] != round_obj.letter:
            reason = "پاسخ اشتباه"
        elif cat in ("اسم", "فامیل"):
            # برای اسم و فامیل فقط حرف اول کافیه
            score = 10
            reason = "صحیح"
        elif not is_valid_word(norm, cat, round_obj.letter):
            reason = "پاسخ اشتباه"
        else:
            score = 10
            reason = "صحیح"
        
        Answer.objects.update_or_create(
            round=round_obj, user=request.user, category=cat,
            defaults={"value": raw, "normalized": norm, "score": score},
        )
        player_answers.append({
            "category": cat,
            "value": raw or "—",
            "normalized": norm,
            "score": score,
            "reason": reason,
        })

    bot_raw, _ = bot_logic.bot_answers_for_round(
        game.categories, round_obj.letter, game.difficulty, round_obj.duration
    )
    bot_answers = []
    for cat in game.categories:
        w = bot_raw.get(cat, "")
        norm = _normalize(w)
        score = 10 if (norm and norm[0] == round_obj.letter) else 0
        BotAnswer.objects.update_or_create(
            round=round_obj, category=cat,
            defaults={"value": w, "normalized": norm, "score": score},
        )
        bot_answers.append({
            "category": cat,
            "value": w or "—",
            "normalized": norm,
            "score": score,
        })

    for i, cat in enumerate(game.categories):
        p_norm = player_answers[i]["normalized"]
        b_norm = bot_answers[i]["normalized"]
        if p_norm and b_norm and p_norm == b_norm:
            player_answers[i]["score"] = 5
            bot_answers[i]["score"] = 5

    player_total = sum(a["score"] for a in player_answers)
    bot_total = sum(a["score"] for a in bot_answers)

    if player_total > bot_total:
        winner = "player"
    elif bot_total > player_total:
        winner = "bot"
    else:
        winner = "tie"

    p = game.players.get(user=request.user)
    p.score += player_total
    p.save(update_fields=["score"])

    return render(request, "game/bot_result.html", {
        "game": game,
        "round": round_obj,
        "categories": game.categories,
        "player_answers": player_answers,
        "bot_answers": bot_answers,
        "player_total": player_total,
        "bot_total": bot_total,
        "winner": winner,
    })





@login_required
def leaderboard_view(request):
    check = _require_complete(request.user)
    if check:
        return check

    now = timezone.now()
    today_start = now.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    week_start = today_start - timedelta(days=7)
    month_start = today_start.replace(day=1)

    def _top(period_start, limit=10):
        rows = (
            Player.objects
            .filter(game__created_at__gte=period_start)
            .values("user_id")
            .annotate(total=Sum("score"), games=Count("id"))
            .order_by("-total")[:limit]
        )
        from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
        User = get_user_model()
        user_ids = [r["user_id"] for r in rows]
        users_map = {u.id: u for u in User.objects.filter(id__in=user_ids)}

        result = []
        for idx, r in enumerate(rows, start=1):
            u = users_map.get(r["user_id"])
            if not u:
                continue
            result.append({
                "rank": idx,
                "user": u,
                "avatar": get_emoji(u.avatar_id),
                "alias": u.alias,
                "total": r["total"] or 0,
                "games": r["games"],
            })
        return result

    daily = _top(today_start)
    weekly = _top(week_start)
    monthly = _top(month_start)

    # رتبه شخصی
    def _my_rank(period_start):
        my_total = (
            Player.objects
            .filter(user=request.user, game__created_at__gte=period_start)
            .aggregate(t=Sum("score"))["t"] or 0
        )
        if my_total == 0:
            return {"total": 0, "rank": "—", "games": 0}

        my_games = Player.objects.filter(
            user=request.user, game__created_at__gte=period_start
        ).count()

        # چند نفر امتیاز بیشتری دارن؟
        better = (
            Player.objects
            .filter(game__created_at__gte=period_start)
            .values("user_id")
            .annotate(t=Sum("score"))
            .filter(t__gt=my_total)
            .count()
        )
        return {"total": my_total, "rank": better + 1, "games": my_games}

    my_daily = _my_rank(today_start)
    my_weekly = _my_rank(week_start)
    my_monthly = _my_rank(month_start)

    return render(request, "game/leaderboard.html", {
        "daily": daily,
        "weekly": weekly,
        "monthly": monthly,
        "my_daily": my_daily,
        "my_weekly": my_weekly,
        "my_monthly": my_monthly,
        "my_avatar": _avatar(request.user),
        "my_alias": request.user.alias,
        "active": "leaderboard",
    })

@login_required
def top_players_view(request):
    """بازیکنان برتر همه دوران."""
    from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
    from django.db.models import Count, Sum

    User = get_user_model()
    top = (
        Player.objects
        .values("user_id")
        .annotate(total=Sum("score"), games=Count("id"))
        .order_by("-total")[:30]
    )

    ids = [t["user_id"] for t in top]
    users = {u.id: u for u in User.objects.filter(id__in=ids)}

    result = []
    for i, t in enumerate(top, 1):
        u = users.get(t["user_id"])
        if not u:
            continue
        result.append({
            "rank": i,
            "user": u,
            "avatar": get_emoji(u.avatar_id),
            "avatar_type": getattr(u, "avatar_type", "emoji"),
            "avatar_id": u.avatar_id,
            "alias": u.alias,
            "total": t["total"] or 0,
            "games": t["games"],
            "level": 1 + (t["total"] or 0) // 100,
        })

    return render(request, "game/top_players.html", {
        "players": result,
        "active": "leaderboard",
    })

@login_required
def match_start(request):
    """ورود به صف انتظار."""
    check = _require_complete(request.user)
    if check:
        return check

    from .models import MatchQueue

    # حذف از صف قبلی
    MatchQueue.objects.filter(user=request.user).delete()

    # اضافه به صف جدید
    MatchQueue.objects.create(user=request.user)

    # چک کن اگه حریف داره
    return redirect("game:match_wait")


@login_required
def match_wait(request):
    """صفحه انتظار."""
    check = _require_complete(request.user)
    if check:
        return check

    from .models import MatchQueue, Game, Player, Round
    from game.bot_data import LETTER_DIFFICULTY
    import random

    # چک کن توی صف هست
    my_queue = MatchQueue.objects.filter(user=request.user).first()
    if not my_queue:
        return redirect("game:rooms")

    # کسی هست که از من زودتر اومده؟
    opponent_queue = MatchQueue.objects.filter(
        joined_at__lt=my_queue.joined_at
    ).exclude(user=request.user).first()

    if opponent_queue:
        # پیدا شد! حالا بازی بساز
        opponent = opponent_queue.user

        # پاک کردن هر دو از صف
        MatchQueue.objects.filter(user__in=[request.user, opponent]).delete()

        # ساخت بازی
        categories = ["اسم", "فامیل", "میوه", "رنگ", "شهر", "کشور",
                     "ماشین", "حیوان", "اشیاء", "غذا"]
        letter = random.choice(list("ابپتثجچحخدذرزژسشصضطظعغفقکگلمنوهی"))

        game = Game.objects.create(
            host=request.user,
            status="waiting",
            categories=categories,
            mode="match",
            difficulty="medium",
            letter_mode="random",
        )
        Player.objects.create(game=game, user=request.user)
        Player.objects.create(game=game, user=opponent)

        return redirect("game:rooms_manage")

    return render(request, "game/match_wait.html", {
        "active": "games",
    })


@login_required
def match_cancel(request):
    """لغو انتظار."""
    from .models import MatchQueue
    MatchQueue.objects.filter(user=request.user).delete()
    return redirect("game:rooms")


@login_required
def match_status(request):
    """چک وضعیت صف (AJAX)."""
    from .models import MatchQueue, Game
    from django.http import JsonResponse

    my_queue = MatchQueue.objects.filter(user=request.user).first()
    if not my_queue:
        return JsonResponse({"status": "cancelled"})

    # اگه توی بازی هستم
    my_game = Game.objects.filter(players__user=request.user, mode="match").order_by("-created_at").first()
    if my_game and my_game.status != "finished":
        return JsonResponse({
            "status": "matched",
            "url": f"/game/room/{my_game.code}/",
        })

    # چند نفر توی صف هستن
    count = MatchQueue.objects.count()
    return JsonResponse({"status": "waiting", "count": count})

def _require_complete(user):
    if not user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect("landing")
    if not user.has_completed_registration or not user.has_chosen_avatar:
        return redirect("accounts:complete_registration")
    return None

@login_required
def rooms_manage(request):
    check = _require_complete(request.user)
    if check:
        return check

    query = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    active_rooms = Game.objects.filter(status="waiting", mode="group").order_by("-created_at")

    if query:
        active_rooms = active_rooms.filter(
            Q(room_name__icontains=query) | Q(host__alias__icontains=query)
        )

    rooms_data = []
    for r in active_rooms:
        count = r.players.count()
        rooms_data.append({
            "obj": r,
            "count": count,
            "is_full": count >= r.max_players,
            "has_password": bool(r.password),
        })

    if request.method == "POST":
        room_name = (request.POST.get("room_name") or "").strip()[:50] or "اتاق بازی"
        password = (request.POST.get("password") or "").strip()[:32]
        max_players = int(request.POST.get("max_players") or 8)
        selected = [c for c in request.POST.getlist("categories") if c in DEFAULT_CATEGORIES]
        if not selected:
            selected = DEFAULT_CATEGORIES

        game = Game.objects.create(
            host=request.user, status="waiting",
            categories=selected, mode="group", password=password,
            room_name=room_name, max_players=max_players
        )
        Player.objects.create(game=game, user=request.user)
        return redirect("game:rooms_manage")

    return render(request, "game/rooms_manage.html", {
        "categories": DEFAULT_CATEGORIES,
        "rooms": rooms_data,
        "query": query,
        "active": "games",
    })

@login_required
def join_room(request, room_code):
    check = _require_complete(request.user)
    if check:
        return check

    room = Game.objects.filter(code=room_code).first()
    if not room:
        return redirect("game:rooms_manage")

    if room.players.count() >= room.max_players:
        return render(request, "game/rooms_manage.html", {
            "categories": DEFAULT_CATEGORIES,
            "rooms": [],
            "error": "ظرفیت این اتاق پر شده است.",
            "active": "games",
        })

    if request.method == "POST":
        password = (request.POST.get("password") or "").strip()
        if room.password and room.password != password:
            return render(request, "game/room_password.html", {
                "room": room,
                "error": "رمز عبور اشتباه است."
            })
        
        Player.objects.get_or_create(game=room, user=request.user)
        return redirect("game:room", room_code=room.code)

    if room.password:
        return render(request, "game/room_password.html", {"room": room})

    Player.objects.get_or_create(game=room, user=request.user)
    return redirect("game:room", room_code=room.code)

@login_required
def room(request, room_code):
    check = _require_complete(request.user)
    if check:
        return check

    room = Game.objects.filter(code=room_code).first()
    if not room:
        return redirect("game:rooms_manage")

    Player.objects.get_or_create(game=room, user=request.user)

    players = []
    for p in room.players.select_related("user"):
        players.append({
            "user_id": p.user.id,
            "alias": p.user.alias,
            "avatar": get_emoji(p.user.avatar_id),
            "is_ready": p.is_ready,
            "score": p.score,
        })

    return render(request, "game/room.html", {
        "room": room,
        "categories": room.categories,
        "players": players,
    })


===== FILE: ./game/models.py =====
import random
import uuid

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models


PERSIAN_LETTERS = list("آابپتثجچحخدذرزژسشصضطظعغفقکگلمنوهی")

DEFAULT_CATEGORIES = [
    "اسم", "فامیل", "میوه", "رنگ", "شهر", "کشور", "ماشین", "حیوان", "اشیاء", "غذا",
    "شغل", "اعضای بدن", "نام گل", "فیلم", "خواننده", "برند", "ورزش", "ابزار", "لباس", 
    "شخصیت کارتونی", "کتاب", "مکان", "گیاه", "مزه", "صدا", "پارچه", "بیماری", 
    "زبان", "عدد", "کلمه انگلیسی"
]


class Game(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("waiting", "waiting"),
        ("in_round", "in_round"),
        ("round_result", "round_result"),
        ("finished", "finished"),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=6, unique=True, editable=False, null=True, blank=True)
    host = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="hosted_games",
    )
    password = models.CharField(max_length=32, blank=True)
    room_name = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default="اتاق بازی")
    max_players = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=8)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=16, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="waiting")
    categories = models.JSONField(default=list)
    mode = models.CharField(max_length=16, default="group")
    difficulty = models.CharField(max_length=8, default="medium")
    letter_mode = models.CharField(max_length=16, default="random")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    finished_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.code:
            self.code = self._generate_code()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @staticmethod
    def _generate_code():
        while True:
            c = str(random.randint(1000, 9999))
            if not Game.objects.filter(code=c).exists():
                return c

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Game {self.code} ({self.status})"


class Player(models.Model):
    game = models.ForeignKey(Game, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="players")
    user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    score = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    is_ready = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("game", "user")


class Round(models.Model):
    game = models.ForeignKey(Game, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="rounds")
    number = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
    letter = models.CharField(max_length=2)
    duration = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=60)
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    stopped_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    stopper = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name="+",
    )

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("game", "number")


class Answer(models.Model):
    round = models.ForeignKey(Round, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="answers")
    user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    category = models.CharField(max_length=32)
    value = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True)
    normalized = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True)
    score = models.IntegerField(default=0)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("round", "user", "category")


class BotAnswer(models.Model):
    round = models.ForeignKey(Round, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="bot_answers")
    category = models.CharField(max_length=32)
    value = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True)
    normalized = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True)
    score = models.IntegerField(default=0)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("round", "category")


class MatchQueue(models.Model):
    """صف انتظار برای بازی رندم."""
    user = models.OneToOneField(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="match_queue",
    )
    difficulty = models.CharField(max_length=8, default="medium")
    joined_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ("joined_at",)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.alias} in queue"

===== FILE: ./game/templates/game/play.html =====
<!doctype html>
<html lang="fa" dir="rtl">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>در حال بازی</title>
  <style>
    body { font-family: Tahoma, sans-serif; background: #0f172a; color: #e2e8f0;
           display: flex; justify-content: center; padding: 30px; }
    .box { background: #1e293b; padding: 30px; border-radius: 12px; width: 700px; }
    .letter { font-size: 60px; color: #facc15; text-align: center;
              margin: 10px 0; font-weight: bold; }
    .timer { font-size: 24px; color: #ef4444; text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; }
    .row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 120px 1fr; gap: 10px;
           margin-bottom: 8px; align-items: center; }
    input[type=text] { padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px; border: none;
                       background: #334155; color: white; font-size: 16px; width: 100%; }
    button { background: #22c55e; color: white; padding: 12px 24px;
             border: none; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 18px; cursor: pointer;
             width: 100%; margin-top: 20px; }
    button:hover { background: #16a34a; }
    .label { text-align: left; color: #94a3b8; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="box">
    <h1>حرف این راند:</h1>
    <div class="letter">{{ round.letter }}</div>
    <div class="timer" id="timer">۶۰ ثانیه</div>

    <form method="post" action="{% url 'game:submit' game.id %}" id="playform">
      {% csrf_token %}
      {% for c in categories %}
        <div class="row">
          <div class="label">{{ c }}</div>
          <input type="text" name="cat_{{ c }}" autocomplete="off">
        </div>
      {% endfor %}
      <button type="submit">✋ استپ!</button>
    </form>
  </div>

  <script>
    const total = {{ round.duration }};
    let left = total;
    const el = document.getElementById("timer");
    const form = document.getElementById("playform");
    const toFa = n => n.toLocaleString('fa-IR');

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      left--;
      el.textContent = toFa(left) + " ثانیه";
      if (left <= 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        form.submit();
      }
    }, 1000);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
  function showBoard(name, btn) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.board').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById('board-' + name).classList.add('active');
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
</script>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
const roomCode = "{{ room.code }}";
const myId = {{ user.id }};

const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
const ws = new WebSocket(`${proto}//${window.location.host}/ws/game/${roomCode}/`);
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);

ws.onmessage = (e) => {
  const msg = JSON.parse(e.data);
  if (msg.type === "state") onState(msg.state);
  else if (msg.type === "chat") onChat(msg);
};

function send(obj) { ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj)); }

function onState(s) {
  const list = s.players.map(p => {
    const me = p.user_id === myId ? " (شما)" : "";
    return `<div class="player-row">
      <div class="player-av ${p.is_ready ? 'ready' : ''}">${p.avatar || '👤'}</div>
      <div class="player-info"><div class="player-name">${p.alias || p.user__alias}${me}</div></div>
      <span class="player-status ${p.is_ready ? 'status-ready' : 'status-wait'}">${p.is_ready ? 'آماده' : 'در انتظار'}</span>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");
  $("playersList").innerHTML = list;

  const allReady = s.players.length >= 2 && s.players.every(p => p.is_ready);
  if ($("btnStart")) {
    $("btnStart").style.display = allReady ? "block" : "none";
  }
}

function onChat(msg) {
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  el.className = "chat-msg";
  el.innerHTML = `<span class="sender">${msg.sender}:</span> ${msg.body}`;
  $("chatMessages").appendChild(el);
  $("chatMessages").scrollTop = $("chatMessages").scrollHeight;
}

$("btnReady").onclick = () => send({ action: "ready" });
if ($("btnStart")) $("btnStart").onclick = () => send({ action: "start" });

$("chatForm").onsubmit = (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const text = $("chatInput").value.trim();
  if (!text) return;
  send({ action: "chat", body: text });
  $("chatInput").value = "";
};
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
// تابع اختصاصی برای انتخاب فقط یک دکمه
function selectRadio(radioId, btnElement, groupName) {
  // تیک زدن رادیو باتن مخفی
  document.getElementById(radioId).checked = true;
  
  // برداشتن کلاس active از بقیه دکمه‌های هم‌گروه
  const parent = btnElement.parentElement;
  parent.querySelectorAll('.pill').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  
  // اضافه کردن کلاس به دکمه کلیک شده
  btnElement.classList.add('active');
}

function moveCat(el, targetContainerId, isExcluded) {
  const cat = el.dataset.cat;
  const targetContainer = document.getElementById(targetContainerId);
  
  const newEl = document.createElement('div');
  newEl.dataset.cat = cat;
  
  if (isExcluded) {
    newEl.className = 'cat-tag';
    newEl.onclick = function() { moveCat(this, 'excludedCats', false); };
  } else {
    newEl.className = 'cat-tag excluded-tag';
    newEl.onclick = function() { moveCat(this, 'activeCats', true); };
  }
  newEl.innerText = cat;
  
  targetContainer.appendChild(newEl);
  el.remove();
  
  const excludedSection = document.getElementById('excludedSection');
  if (document.getElementById('excludedCats').children.length > 0) {
    excludedSection.style.display = 'block';
  } else {
    excludedSection.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

document.getElementById('botForm').onsubmit = function() {
  const hiddenContainer = document.getElementById('hiddenInputs');
  hiddenContainer.innerHTML = '';
  
  const activeElements = document.querySelectorAll('#activeCats .cat-tag');
  if (activeElements.length === 0) {
    alert('حداقل یک دسته باید فعال باشد!');
    return false;
  }
  
  activeElements.forEach(cat => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'hidden';
    input.name = 'categories';
    input.value = cat.dataset.cat;
    hiddenContainer.appendChild(input);
  });
};
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
  <div class="wait-box">
    <div class="spinner"></div>
    <h1>در حال جستجوی حریف…</h1>
    <p class="sub">کمی صبر کن. وقتی یه نفر پیدا شه،<br>خودکار به اتاق بازی می‌ری.</p>
    <div class="count" id="countBox">در انتظار…</div>
    <br>
    <a href="/game/match/cancel/" class="cancel-btn">✕ لغو</a>
  </div>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
  let checks = 0;
  const interval = setInterval(checkStatus, 2000);

  function checkStatus() {
    checks++;
    if (checks > 60) {
      clearInterval(interval);
      alert('حریفی پیدا نشد. دوباره تلاش کن.');
      window.location.href = '/game/rooms/';
      return;
    }
    fetch('/game/match/status/')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (data.status === 'matched') {
          clearInterval(interval);
          window.location.href = data.url;
        } else if (data.status === 'cancelled') {
          clearInterval(interval);
          window.location.href = '/game/rooms/';
        } else if (data.count) {
          document.getElementById('countBox').textContent = data.count + ' نفر در صف';
        }
      })
      .catch(e => console.log(e));
  }
</script>
{% endblock %}

===== FILE: ./game/templates/game/bot_play.html =====
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% block title %}در حال بازی{% endblock %}

{% block extra_css %}
<style>

  }
  .answer-row .label { width: 80px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; }

  }

<script>
  let total = {{ duration }};
  let left = total;
  const textEl = document.getElementById('timerText');
  const circle = document.getElementById('timerProgress');
  const form = document.getElementById('form');
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 36;
  circle.style.strokeDasharray = circumference;

  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    textEl.textContent = left;
    circle.style.strokeDashoffset = circumference - (left / total) * circumference;
    if (left <= 10) { circle.style.stroke = '#ef4444'; textEl.style.color = '#ef4444'; } 
    else if (left <= total / 2) { circle.style.stroke = '#eab308'; textEl.style.color = '#eab308'; }
    if (left <= 0) { clearInterval(timer); form.submit(); }
  }, 1000);
</script>

===== FILE: ./game/consumers.py =====
import asyncio
import random
from channels.db import database_sync_to_async
from channels.generic.websocket import AsyncJsonWebsocketConsumer
from django.utils import timezone
from .models import Game, Player, Round, Answer
from accounts.avatars import get_emoji

class GameConsumer(AsyncJsonWebsocketConsumer):
    async def connect(self):
        self.user = self.scope["user"]
        if not self.user.is_authenticated:
            await self.close()
            return

        self.room_code = self.scope["url_route"]["kwargs"]["room_code"]
        room = await self._get_room()
        if not room:
            await self.close()
            return

        self.room_id = str(room.id)
        self.group_name = f"game_{self.room_id}"

        await self.channel_layer.group_add(self.group_name, self.channel_name)
        await self.accept()
        await self._add_player()
        await self._broadcast_state()

    async def disconnect(self, close_code):
        if hasattr(self, "group_name"):
            await self.channel_layer.group_discard(self.group_name, self.channel_name)

    async def receive_json(self, content):
        action = content.get("action")
        if action == "ready":
            await self._set_ready(True)
            await self._broadcast_state()
        elif action == "unready":
            await self._set_ready(False)
            await self._broadcast_state()
        elif action == "start":
            await self._start_game()
        elif action == "submit":
            await self._handle_submit(content.get("answers", {}))

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _get_room(self):
        return Game.objects.filter(code=self.room_code).first()

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _add_player(self):
        room = Game.objects.get(id=self.room_id)
        Player.objects.get_or_create(game=room, user=self.user)

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _set_ready(self, status):
        Player.objects.filter(game_id=self.room_id, user=self.user).update(is_ready=status)

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _state_payload(self):
        room = Game.objects.get(id=self.room_id)
        players = []
        for p in room.players.select_related("user"):
            players.append({
                "user_id": p.user.id,
                "alias": p.user.alias,
                "avatar": get_emoji(p.user.avatar_id),
                "is_ready": p.is_ready,
                "score": p.score,
            })
        rnd = room.rounds.order_by("-number").first()
        return {
            "status": room.status,
            "host_id": room.host_id,
            "players": players,
            "round_number": rnd.number if rnd else 0,
            "letter": rnd.letter if rnd else "",
            "categories": room.categories,
        }

    async def _broadcast_state(self):
        state = await self._state_payload()
        await self.channel_layer.group_send(
            self.group_name, {"type": "ev.state", "state": state}
        )

    async def ev_state(self, event):
        await self.send_json({"type": "state", "state": event["state"]})

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _create_round(self):
        room = Game.objects.get(id=self.room_id)
        if room.host != self.user:
            return None
        
        if not all(p.is_ready for p in room.players.all()) or room.players.count() < 2:
            return None

        letter = random.choice(list("ابپتثجچحخدذرزژسشصضطظعغفقکگلمنوهی"))
        rnd = Round.objects.create(game=room, number=room.rounds.count() + 1, letter=letter, duration=60)
        room.status = "in_round"
        room.save()
        return rnd

    async def _start_game(self):
        rnd = await self._create_round()
        if rnd:
            await self.channel_layer.group_send(
                self.group_name,
                {"type": "ev.round_start", "letter": rnd.letter, "duration": rnd.duration},
            )
            await self._broadcast_state()

    async def ev_round_start(self, event):
        await self.send_json({"type": "round_start", "letter": event["letter"], "duration": event["duration"]})

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _handle_submit_db(self, answers):
        room = Game.objects.get(id=self.room_id)
        rnd = room.rounds.order_by("-number").last()
        if not rnd or rnd.stopped_at:
            return None

        for cat, val in answers.items():
            norm = val.strip()
            score = 10 if norm.startswith(rnd.letter) else 0
            Answer.objects.update_or_create(
                round=rnd, user=self.user, category=cat,
                defaults={"value": val, "normalized": norm, "score": score}
            )

        first_stop = False
        if not rnd.stopped_at:
            rnd.stopped_at = timezone.now()
            rnd.stopper = self.user
            rnd.save()
            first_stop = True

        return first_stop

    async def _handle_submit(self, answers):
        first_stop = await self._handle_submit_db(answers)
        if first_stop:
            await self.channel_layer.group_send(
                self.group_name,
                {"type": "ev.stopping", "stopper": self.user.alias},
            )
            await asyncio.sleep(10)
            results = await self._evaluate_round()
            await self.channel_layer.group_send(
                self.group_name,
                {"type": "ev.round_result", "results": results},
            )
            await self._broadcast_state()

    async def ev_stopping(self, event):
        await self.send_json({"type": "stopping", "stopper": event["stopper"]})

    @database_sync_to_async
    def _evaluate_round(self):
        room = Game.objects.get(id=self.room_id)
        rnd = room.rounds.order_by("-number").last()
        answers = list(rnd.answers.select_related("user"))

        cat_counts = {}
        for a in answers:
            if a.normalized.startswith(rnd.letter):
                cat_counts[a.normalized] = cat_counts.get(a.normalized, 0) + 1

        player_totals = {}
        for a in answers:
            score = 0
            if a.normalized.startswith(rnd.letter):
                if cat_counts.get(a.normalized, 0) == 1:
                    score = 20
                else:
                    score = 5
            a.score = score
            a.save()
            player_totals[a.user_id] = player_totals.get(a.user_id, 0) + score

        for p in room.players.all():
            p.score += player_totals.get(p.user_id, 0)
            p.save()

        room.status = "round_result"
        room.save()

        return [{"alias": p.user.alias, "score": p.score} for p in room.players.select_related("user")]

===== FILE: ./game/routing.py =====
from django.urls import re_path

from . import consumers

websocket_urlpatterns = [
    re_path(r"^ws/game/(?P<room_code>\d{4})/$", consumers.GameConsumer.as_asgi()),
]
===== FILE: ./game/bot.py =====
# game/bot.py
"""موتور بات: پاسخ‌دهی هوشمند با زمان‌بندی متغیر بر اساس سختی و حرف."""

import random

from .bot_data import words_for, LETTER_DIFFICULTY

HARD_LETTERS = {"ث", "ض", "ظ", "ژ", "ط", "غ", "ذ"}
MEDIUM_LETTERS = {"ح", "خ", "ص", "ق", "ع", "ک", "گ", "چ", "پ", "ش", "ز", "و", "ه"}

ALL_LETTERS = list(LETTER_DIFFICULTY.keys())


def letter_weight(letter):
    if letter in HARD_LETTERS:
        return 1.4
    if letter in MEDIUM_LETTERS:
        return 1.2
    return 1.0


def bot_pick_letter(difficulty):
    """بات یک حرف بر اساس سختی بازی انتخاب می‌کند."""
    if difficulty == "easy":
        pool = [l for l in ALL_LETTERS if LETTER_DIFFICULTY[l] == 1]
    elif difficulty == "medium":
        pool = [l for l in ALL_LETTERS if LETTER_DIFFICULTY[l] <= 2]
    else:
        pool = ALL_LETTERS[:]
    return random.choice(pool)


def bot_answer_for(category, letter, difficulty):
    """بات یک کلمه برای دسته و حرف انتخاب می‌کند. اگر نتوانست، خالی."""
    words = words_for(category, letter)
    if not words:
        return ""
    fail_rate = {"easy": 0.35, "medium": 0.18, "hard": 0.07}.get(difficulty, 0.18)
    if random.random() < fail_rate:
        return ""
    return random.choice(words)


def bot_answers_for_round(categories, letter, difficulty, max_seconds):
    """پاسخ‌های بات + زمان صرف شده."""
    base_ratio = {"easy": 0.85, "medium": 0.60, "hard": 0.35}.get(difficulty, 0.60)
    ratio = base_ratio * letter_weight(letter)
    ratio = max(0.15, min(0.9, ratio))
    elapsed = max_seconds * ratio * random.uniform(0.85, 1.15)

    answers = {}
    used = 0.0
    per_word = elapsed / max(len(categories), 1)
    for cat in categories:
        if used + per_word > elapsed:
            answers[cat] = ""
        else:
            answers[cat] = bot_answer_for(cat, letter, difficulty)
            used += per_word
    return answers, elapsed

def words_for(category: str, letter: str):
    cat = WORD_DB.get(category, {})
    return cat.get(letter, [])

def is_valid_word(word: str, category: str, letter: str) -> bool:
    if not word:
        return False
    w = word.strip().replace("\u200c", "").replace(" ", "")
    if not w or w[0] != letter:
        return False
    candidates = words_for(category, letter)
    normalized = [c.replace("\u200c", "").replace(" ", "") for c in candidates]
    return w in normalized or w.lower() in [c.lower() for c in normalized]

# ============================================================
# بازنویسی تابع اعتبارسنجی با نرمال‌سازی دقیق‌تر
# ============================================================
def _norm2(t):
    if not t:
        return ""
    return t.strip().replace("\u200c", "").replace(" ", "").replace("ي", "ی").replace("ك", "ک").lower()

def is_valid_word(word, category, letter):
    w = _norm2(word)
    if not w or w[0] != letter:
        return False
    candidates = words_for(category, letter)
    return w in [_norm2(c) for c in candidates]

===== FILE: ./manage.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python
import os
import sys

def main():
    os.environ.setdefault("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "config.settings")
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
